\\treturn acpi_dev_found(\\\"GOOG0004\\\") || acpi_dev_found(\\\"GOOG000C\\\");\\n965: }\\n966: \\n967: /*\\n968:  * Windows 8 and newer no longer use the ACPI video interface, so it often\\n969:  * does not work. So on win8+ systems prefer native brightness control.\\n970:  * Chromebooks should always prefer native backlight control.\\n971:  */\\n972: static bool prefer_native_over_acpi_video(void)\\n973: {\\n974: \\treturn acpi_osi_is_win8() || google_cros_ec_present();\\n975: }\\n976: \\n977: /*\\n978:  * Determine which type of backlight interface to use on this system,\\n979:  * First check cmdline, then dmi quirks, then do autodetect.\\n980:  */\\n981: enum acpi_backlight_type __acpi_video_get_backlight_type(bool native, bool *auto_detect)\\n982: {\\n983: \\tstatic DEFINE_MUTEX(init_mutex);\\n984: \\tstatic bool nvidia_wmi_ec_present;\\n985: \\tstatic bool apple_gmux_present;\\n986: \\tstatic bool dell_uart_present;\\n987: \\tstatic bool native_available;\\n988: \\tstatic bool init_done;\\n989: \\tstatic long video_caps;\\n990: \\n991: \\t/* Parse cmdline, dmi and acpi only once */\\n992: \\tmutex_lock(&init_mutex);\\n993: \\tif (!init_done) {\\n994: \\t\\tacpi_video_parse_cmdline();\\n995: \\t\\tdmi_check_system(video_detect_dmi_table);\\n996: \\t\\tacpi_walk_namespace(ACPI_TYPE_DEVICE, ACPI_ROOT_OBJECT,\\n997: \\t\\t\\t\\t    ACPI_UINT32_MAX, find_video, NULL,\\n998: \\t\\t\\t\\t    &video_caps, NULL);\\n999: \\t\\tnvidia_wmi_ec_present = nvidia_wmi_ec_supported();\\n1000: \\t\\tapple_gmux_present = apple_gmux_detect(NULL, NULL);\\n1001: \\t\\tdell_uart_present = acpi_dev_present(\\\"DELL0501\\\", NULL, -1);\\n1002: \\t\\tinit_done = true;\\n1003: \\t}\\n1004: \\tif (native)\\n1005: \\t\\tnative_available = true;\\n1006: \\tmutex_unlock(&init_mutex);\\n1007: \\n1008: \\tif (auto_detect)\\n1009: \\t\\t*auto_detect = false;\\n1010: \\n1011: \\t/*\\n1012: \\t * The below heuristics / detection steps are in order of descending\\n1013: \\t * presedence. The commandline takes presedence over anything else.\\n1014: \\t */\\n1015: \\tif (acpi_backlight_cmdline != acpi_backlight_undef)\\n1016: \\t\\treturn acpi_backlight_cmdline;\\n1017: \\n1018: \\t/* DMI quirks override any autodetection. */\\n1019: \\tif (acpi_backlight_dmi != acpi_backlight_undef)\\n1020: \\t\\treturn acpi_backlight_dmi;\\n1021: \\n1022: \\tif (auto_detect)\\n1023: \\t\\t*auto_detect = true;\\n1024: \\n1025: \\t/* Special cases such as nvidia_wmi_ec and apple gmux. */\\n1026: \\tif (nvidia_wmi_ec_present)\\n1027: \\t\\treturn acpi_backlight_nvidia_wmi_ec;\\n1028: \\n1029: \\tif (apple_gmux_present)\\n1030: \\t\\treturn acpi_backlight_apple_gmux;\\n1031: \\n1032: \\tif (dell_uart_present)\\n1033: \\t\\treturn acpi_backlight_dell_uart;\\n1034: \\n1035: \\t/* Use ACPI video if available, except when native should be preferred. */\\n1036: \\tif ((video_caps & ACPI_VIDEO_BACKLIGHT) &&\\n1037: \\t     !(native_available && prefer_native_over_acpi_video()))\\n1038: \\t\\treturn acpi_backlight_video;\\n1039: \\n1040: \\t/* Use native if available */\\n1041: \\tif (native_available)\\n1042: \\t\\treturn acpi_backlight_native;\\n1043: \\n1044: \\t/*\\n1045: \\t * The vendor specific BIOS interfaces are only necessary for\\n1046: \\t * laptops from before ~2008.\\n1047: \\t *\\n1048: \\t * For laptops from ~2008 till ~2023 this point is never reached\\n1049: \\t * because on those (video_caps & ACPI_VIDEO_BACKLIGHT) above is true.\\n1050: \\t *\\n1051: \\t * Laptops from after ~2023 no longer support ACPI_VIDEO_BACKLIGHT,\\n1052: \\t * if this point is reached on those, this likely means that\\n1053: \\t * the GPU kms driver which sets native_available has not loaded yet.\\n1054: \\t *\\n1055: \\t * Returning acpi_backlight_vendor in this case is known to sometimes\\n1056: \\t * cause a non working vendor specific /sys/class/backlight device to\\n1057: \\t * get
